It prints\nbore-up, then flips onto the stem so the solid floor becomes the crown.\n\n## Design notes\n\n- Stem is 8.0 mm across, 6.5 mm across the flat, 12 mm proud of the body\n  (`measurements.toml`).\n- Bore is those two numbers plus 0.5 mm so a 0.3 mm-grown stem slides in\n  and a 1.0 mm-grown stem jams, and the flat carries torque.\n- Three lobes on a 30 mm hub give wet-hand grip: 30 mm at the waist, lobes\n  reaching 21 mm from the centerline.\n- 16 mm tall, 4 mm floor, D-cavity open at the print top. In use the floor\n  is the face you see.\n\n## Don't\n\n- Don't use a round bore: it will not transmit torque.\n- Don't polish the D-bore. Mating geometry stays sharp.\n- Don't add a lead-in chamfer on the bore mouth.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-24: first cut, 3-lobe knob with D-bore from stem measurements.\n"},"content":[],"locations":[]}

It prints\nbore-up, then flips onto the stem so the solid floor becomes the crown.\n\n## Design notes\n\n- Stem is 8.0 mm across, 6.5 mm across the flat, 12 mm proud of the body\n  (`measurements.toml`).\n- Bore is those two numbers plus 0.5 mm so a 0.3 mm-grown stem slides in\n  and a 1.0 mm-grown stem jams, and the flat carries torque.\n- Three lobes on a 30 mm hub give wet-hand grip: 30 mm at the waist, lobes\n  reaching 21 mm from the centerline.\n- 16 mm tall, 4 mm floor, D-cavity open at the print top. {"type":"tool_call","toolCallId":"call-3430e864-6a76-412c-a29e-40643ea4f10c-24","title":"run_terminal_command","kind":"execute","status":"pending","toolName":"run_terminal_command","rawInput":{"command":"<home>/.local/share/uv/tools/nurb/bin/python << 'PY'\nfrom math import hypot\nfrom nurb import *\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, \".\")\n\n# Import the part function by loading the file\nimport importlib.util\nspec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"valve_knob\", \"parts/valve_knob.py\")\nmod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\nspec.loader.exec_module(mod)\n\nknob = mod.valve_knob()\nprint(\"knob type\", type(knob), \"volume\", knob.volume)\nbb = knob.bounding_box()\nprint(\"bbox\", bb.min, bb.max)\ntop = bb.max.Z\nprint(\"top\", top, \"insert_to\", top - 10.0)\n\ndef d_stem(diameter, across_flat, z0, z1):\n    r = diameter / 2.0\n    flat_x = -r + across_flat\n    h = z1 - z0\n    cyl = Pos(0, 0, z0) * Cylinder(r, h, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))\n    slab = Pos(flat_x, 0, z0 - 1) * Box(\n        r * 2 + 4, r * 2 + 4, h + 4, align=(Align.MIN, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN)\n    )\n    return cyl - slab\n\ndef collide(a, b):\n    try:\n        inter = a.intersect(b)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(\"intersect error\", e)\n        # fallback\n        inter = a & b\n    if inter is None:\n        return 0.0\n    # intersect may return Compound/ShapeList\n    vol = getattr(inter, \"volume\", None)\n    if vol is None:\n        try:\n            vol = sum(s.volume for s in inter)\n        except Exception:\n            print(\"no volume\", type(inter), inter)\n            return -1\n    return vol\n\nshaft_d = 8.0\nshaft_f = 6.5\nz_bottom = top - 10.0\nz_top = top + 5.0  # {"type":"tool_call_update","toolCallId":"call-b38a6335-cb58-43fb-b1fc-fd22d9acd19b-27","status":"completed","content":[{"type":"content","content":{"type":"text","text":"85→def measured(name, start=None):\n    \"\"\"A measured value, in mm.\n\n    Raises when it is not on file, when it has no provenance, or when it is recorded in\n    units this does not work in. All three are cases where continuing would produce\n90→    geometry that looks right and is wrong.\n\n    A value marked `provisional = true` is returned like any other. It is a real number\n    that builds a real part; what it is not is measured, and `nurb check` says so until\n    somebody picks up a caliper.\n    \"\"\"\n    start = pathlib.Path(start).resolve() if start else _caller_dir(2)\n    path = _find(start)\n    if path is None:\n        raise MeasurementError(\n100→            f\"no {FILENAME} above {start}, so there is nothing measured to ask for. \"\n            f\"Measure {name!r}, then record it:\\n\\n\"\n            f'  [{name}]\\n  value = 0\\n  unit = \"mm\"\\n  how = \"how you measured it\"\\n\\n'\n            f\"If there is nobody to ask right now, add `provisional = true` and put \"\n            f\"your best guess in. `nurb check` will keep reminding you.\"\n        )\n    book = _read(path)\n    if name not in book:\n        have = \", \".join(sorted(book)) or \"nothing yet\"\n        raise MeasurementError(\n110→            f\"nothing measured called {name!r} in {path.name}. have: {have}. \"\n            f\"Ask for the measurement rather than guessing one: a wrong number here \"\n            f\"builds, checks clean, and prints.\"\n        )\n    entry = book[name]\n    if \"value\" not in entry:\n        raise MeasurementError(f\"{path.name}: {name} has no value\")\n    if \"how\" not in entry:\n        raise MeasurementError(\n            f\"{path.name}: {name} does not say how it was measured.
